# 第08章-附录-评估脚本实现

import torch
from collections import Counter

class SIDEvaluator:
    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=5):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers

    def reconstruction_mse(self, original, recon):
        return np.mean((original - recon) ** 2)

    def cosine_similarity(self, original, recon):
        cos = (original * recon).sum(axis=-1) / (
            np.linalg.norm(original, axis=-1) * np.linalg.norm(recon, axis=-1) + 1e-8
        )
        return cos.mean()

    def collision_rate(self, sids):
        sid_tuples = [tuple(s.tolist()) for s in sids]
        counter = Counter(sid_tuples)
        n_collisions = sum(c - 1 for c in counter.values() if c > 1)
        return n_collisions / len(sids)

    def coverage(self, sids):
        used = set()
        for sid in sids:
            for c in sid:
                used.add(c)
        return len(used) / (self.num_codes * self.num_layers)

    def evaluate(self, original, recon, sids):
        return {
            'mse': self.reconstruction_mse(original, recon),
            'cosine': self.cosine_similarity(original, recon),
            'collision_rate': self.collision_rate(sids),
            'coverage': self.coverage(sids),
        }
```

## A.2 基础评估类

### A.2.1 SIDEvaluator 完整实现

```python
"""完整 SID 评估脚本 - 工业级"""
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from collections import Counter
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional, List
import json
from datetime import datetime


class SIDEvaluator:
    """SID 综合评估器 (生产级)"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4, device='cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.device = device

        # 初始化子评估器
        from sid_reconstruction_metrics import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        from sid_collision_coverage_metrics import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        from sid_info_theory_metrics import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator

        self.recon_evaluator = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers, device)
        self.cc_evaluator = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers)
        self.info_evaluator = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers)

    def evaluate(self, model, dataloader, popularity: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
                 orig_emb: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
                 recon_emb: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
                 sids: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> Dict:
        """主评估入口"""
        result = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'config': {
                'num_codes': self.num_codes,
                'num_layers': self.num_layers,
                'device': self.device,
            }
        }

        # 1. 重建指标
        if orig_emb is None or recon_emb is None:
            if model is not None:
                orig_emb, recon_emb, sids = self._compute_recon(model, dataloader)

        result['reconstruction'] = self.recon_evaluator.evaluate_recon(orig_emb, recon_emb)

        # 2. 碰撞/覆盖率
        if sids is not None:
            result['collision_coverage'] = self.cc_evaluator.evaluate(sids, popularity)

        # 3. 信息论指标
        if sids is not None:
            result['info_theory'] = self.info_evaluator.evaluate(sids, orig_emb, recon_emb)

        # 4. 综合评分
        result['overall_score'] = self._compute_overall_score(result)

        # 5. 评估结论
        result['verdict'] = self._compute_verdict(result)

        return result

    def _compute_recon(self, model, dataloader):
        """从模型计算重建"""
        model.eval()
        all_orig = []
        all_recon = []
        all_sids = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in dataloader:
                x = batch[0].to(self.device)
                if len(batch) > 1:
                    # 多任务
                    out = model(x, *batch[1:])
                else:
                    out = model(x)
                x_recon, sids = out[0], out[1]
                all_orig.append(x.cpu())
                all_recon.append(x_recon.cpu())
                if sids is not None:
                    all_sids.append(sids.cpu())

        orig = torch.cat(all_orig, dim=0)
        recon = torch.cat(all_recon, dim=0)
        sids_cat = torch.cat(all_sids, dim=0) if all_sids else None
        return orig, recon, sids_cat

    def _compute_overall_score(self, result):
        """综合评分 0-100"""
        score = 0
        weight_sum = 0

        if 'reconstruction' in result:
            cos = result['reconstruction'].get('cosine_mean', 0)
            score += 30 * cos
            weight_sum += 30

        if 'collision_coverage' in result:
            cr = result['collision_coverage']['overall']['collision_rate']
            cov = result['collision_coverage']['overall']['coverage']
            score += 20 * (1 - min(cr * 5, 1))  # 5% 碰撞率扣 25 分
            score += 15 * cov
            weight_sum += 35

        if 'info_theory' in result:
            H = result['info_theory']['overall']['entropy_ratio']
            score += 20 * H
            weight_sum += 20

        return score / weight_sum * 100 if weight_sum > 0 else 0

    def _compute_verdict(self, result):
        """评估结论"""
        score = result.get('overall_score', 0)
        if score > 85:
            return 'EXCELLENT'
        elif score > 70:
            return 'GOOD'
        elif score > 50:
            return 'WARNING'
        else:
            return 'CRITICAL'

    def save_report(self, result, path):
        """保存报告"""
        # 转 numpy 为 list (JSON 序列化)
        def convert(o):
            if isinstance(o, (np.integer, np.floating, np.ndarray)):
                return o.tolist() if isinstance(o, np.ndarray) else float(o)
            if isinstance(o, torch.Tensor):
                return o.tolist()
            return str(o)

        with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(result, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=convert)
```

## A.3 重建指标模块

```python
"""重建指标模块"""
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional
from collections import Counter


class SIDReconstructionEvaluator:
    """SID 重建指标"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4, device='cpu'):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.device = device

    def reconstruction_mse(self, original, recon):
        """MSE / RMSE / MAE / PSNR"""
        mse = F.mse_loss(recon, original).item()
        rmse = mse ** 0.5
        mae = F.l1_loss(recon, original).item()
        max_val = original.abs().max().item()
        psnr = 20 * np.log10(max_val / (rmse + 1e-8))
        return {'mse': mse, 'rmse': rmse, 'mae': mae, 'psnr': psnr}

    def cosine_metrics(self, original, recon):
        """Cosine 全套"""
        cos = F.cosine_similarity(original, recon, dim=-1)
        cos_np = cos.cpu().numpy()
        return {
            'cosine_mean': cos_np.mean(),
            'cosine_std': cos_np.std(),
            'cosine_min': cos_np.min(),
            'cosine_max': cos_np.max(),
            'cosine_p10': float(np.percentile(cos_np, 10)),
            'cosine_p50': float(np.percentile(cos_np, 50)),
            'cosine_p90': float(np.percentile(cos_np, 90)),
            'cosine_p99': float(np.percentile(cos_np, 99)),
            'frac_below_0.9': float((cos_np < 0.9).mean()),
            'frac_below_0.95': float((cos_np < 0.95).mean()),
        }

    def neighborhood_preservation(self, original, recon, k_values=(5, 10, 50)):
        """邻居保持率"""
        n = original.shape[0]
        if n > 5000:
            idx = torch.randperm(n)[:5000]
            original = original[idx]
            recon = recon[idx]
            n = 5000

        sim_orig = (original @ original.T).fill_diagonal_(-1e9)
        sim_recon = (recon @ recon.T).fill_diagonal_(-1e9)

        results = {}
        for k in k_values:
            topk_orig = sim_orig.topk(k, dim=-1).indices.cpu().numpy()
            topk_recon = sim_recon.topk(k, dim=-1).indices.cpu().numpy()

            preservation = []
            for i in range(n):
                inter = len(set(topk_orig[i]) & set(topk_recon[i]))
                preservation.append(inter / k)
            results[f'np@{k}'] = float(np.mean(preservation))
        return results

    def rank_aware_recall(self, original, recon, k=10):
        """Top-K 检索重建率"""
        n = original.shape[0]
        sim_orig = (original @ original.T).fill_diagonal_(-1e9)
        sim_recon = (recon @ recon.T).fill_diagonal_(-1e9)

        topk_orig = set(sim_orig.topk(k, dim=-1).indices[0].cpu().tolist())
        topk_recon = set(sim_recon.topk(k, dim=-1).indices[0].cpu().tolist())

        return len(topk_orig & topk_recon) / k

    def triplet_accuracy(self, original, recon, n_sample=1000):
        """三元组准确率"""
        n = original.shape[0]
        idx = torch.randperm(n)[:n_sample]
        x_s = original[idx]
        x_r = recon[idx]

        neg_idx = torch.randint(0, n, (n_sample,))
        x_neg = original[neg_idx]

        pos_dist = F.pairwise_distance(x_s, x_r)
        neg_dist = F.pairwise_distance(x_s, x_neg)

        return float((pos_dist < neg_dist).float().mean())

    def stratified_metrics(self, original, recon, popularity):
        """按热度分层"""
        mse_per_item = ((original - recon) ** 2).mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()
        cos_per_item = F.cosine_similarity(original, recon, dim=-1).cpu().numpy()

        p33, p67 = np.percentile(popularity, [33, 67])
        results = {}
        for name, mask in [
            ('head', popularity >= p67),
            ('body', (popularity >= p33) & (popularity < p67)),
            ('tail', popularity < p33)
        ]:
            if mask.sum() > 0:
                results[name] = {
                    'mse': float(mse_per_item[mask].mean()),
                    'cosine': float(cos_per_item[mask].mean()),
                    'count': int(mask.sum()),
                }
        return results

    def evaluate_recon(self, original, recon, popularity=None):
        """重建评估主入口"""
        result = {}
        result.update(self.reconstruction_mse(original, recon))
        result.update(self.cosine_metrics(original, recon))
        result.update(self.neighborhood_preservation(original, recon))
        result['rank_aware_recall@10'] = self.rank_aware_recall(original, recon, k=10)
        result['triplet_accuracy'] = self.triplet_accuracy(original, recon)

        if popularity is not None:
            result['stratified'] = self.stratified_metrics(original, recon, popularity)

        return result
```

## A.4 碰撞/覆盖率模块

```python
"""碰撞/覆盖率模块"""
import numpy as np
import torch
from collections import Counter
from typing import Dict, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator:
    """SID 碰撞率/覆盖率"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers

    def full_collision_rate(self, sids):
        sid_tuples = [tuple(s.tolist()) for s in sids]
        counter = Counter(sid_tuples)
        n_coll = sum(c - 1 for c in counter.values() if c > 1)
        return n_coll / len(sids)

    def coverage(self, sids):
        used = set()
        for sid in sids:
            for c in sid:
                used.add(c)
        return len(used) / (self.num_codes * self.num_layers)

    def effective_coverage(self, sids, min_usage=5):
        used_counts = {}
        for sid in sids:
            for c in sid:
                used_counts[c] = used_counts.get(c, 0) + 1
        effective = sum(1 for v in used_counts.values() if v >= min_usage)
        return effective / (self.num_codes * self.num_layers)

    def top_k_concentration(self, sids, k=10):
        counts = np.bincount(sids.flatten(), minlength=self.num_codes).astype(float)
        sorted_counts = np.sort(counts)[::-1]
        return sorted_counts[:k].sum() / counts.sum()

    def max_collision_group(self, sids):
        counter = Counter(tuple(s.tolist()) for s in sids)
        return max(counter.values())

    def per_layer_metrics(self, sids):
        results = {}
        for l in range(self.num_layers):
            counter = Counter(sids[:, l].tolist())
            n_coll = sum(c - 1 for c in counter.values() if c > 1)
            cr = n_coll / len(sids)
            cov = len(counter) / self.num_codes
            results[f'layer{l}'] = {
                'collision_rate': cr,
                'coverage': cov,
            }
        return results

    def prefix_collision(self, sids, prefix_len):
        prefixes = [tuple(s[:prefix_len].tolist()) for s in sids]
        counter = Counter(prefixes)
        n_coll = sum(c - 1 for c in counter.values() if c > 1)
        return n_coll / len(sids)

    def stratified_analysis(self, sids, popularity):
        p33, p67 = np.percentile(popularity, [33, 67])
        results = {}
        for name, mask in [
            ('head', popularity >= p67),
            ('body', (popularity >= p33) & (popularity < p67)),
            ('tail', popularity < p33)
        ]:
            if mask.sum() > 0:
                sids_sub = sids[mask]
                results[name] = {
                    'count': int(mask.sum()),
                    'collision_rate': self.full_collision_rate(sids_sub),
                    'coverage': self.coverage(sids_sub),
                }
        return results

    def evaluate(self, sids, popularity=None):
        result = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'overall': {
                'collision_rate': self.full_collision_rate(sids),
                'coverage': self.coverage(sids),
                'effective_coverage': self.effective_coverage(sids),
                'top10_concentration': self.top_k_concentration(sids, k=10),
                'max_collision_group': self.max_collision_group(sids),
            },
            'layer': self.per_layer_metrics(sids),
        }

        for prefix_len in range(1, self.num_layers):
            result.setdefault('prefix', {})[prefix_len] = {
                'collision_rate': self.prefix_collision(sids, prefix_len),
            }

        if popularity is not None:
            result['stratified'] = self.stratified_analysis(sids, popularity)

        return result
```

## A.5 码本利用率模块

```python
"""码本利用率模块"""
import numpy as np
import torch
from typing import Dict


class SIDCodebookUsageEvaluator:
    """码本利用率"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers

    @staticmethod
    def gini(x):
        x = np.sort(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))
        n = len(x)
        if x.sum() == 0: return 0
        cum = np.cumsum(x)
        return (n + 1 - 2 * np.sum(cum) / cum[-1]) / n

    @staticmethod
    def entropy(p, eps=1e-10):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
        p = p / (p.sum() + eps)
        p = p[p > 0]
        return -(p * np.log(p)).sum()

    def usage_distribution(self, sids):
        return np.bincount(sids.flatten(), minlength=self.num_codes).astype(float)

    def per_layer_entropy(self, sids):
        results = {}
        for l in range(self.num_layers):
            counts = np.bincount(sids[:, l], minlength=self.num_codes).astype(float)
            p = counts / counts.sum()
            H = self.entropy(p)
            H_max = np.log(self.num_codes)
            results[f'layer{l}'] = {
                'entropy': H,
                'entropy_max': H_max,
                'entropy_ratio': H / H_max,
                'gini': self.gini(counts),
                'max_usage_ratio': float(counts.max() / counts.sum()),
                'top10_concentration': float(np.sort(counts)[-10:].sum() / counts.sum()),
            }
        return results

    def overall_entropy(self, sids):
        counts = np.bincount(sids.flatten(), minlength=self.num_codes).astype(float)
        H = self.entropy(counts)
        H_max = np.log(self.num_codes * self.num_layers)
        return {
            'entropy': H,
            'entropy_max': H_max,
            'entropy_ratio': H / H_max,
            'gini': self.gini(counts),
            'effective_codebook_size': float(np.exp(H)),
            'participation_ratio': float(1 / ((counts/counts.sum())**2).sum()),
        }

    def evaluate(self, sids):
        return {
            'per_layer': self.per_layer_entropy(sids),
            'overall': self.overall_entropy(sids),
            'usage_distribution': self.usage_distribution(sids),
        }
```

## A.6 信息论指标模块

```python
"""信息论指标模块"""
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Dict


class SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator:
    """SID 信息论指标"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers

    @staticmethod
    def kl_divergence(p, q, eps=1e-10):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float) + eps
        q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float) + eps
        p = p / p.sum()
        q = q / q.sum()
        return (p * np.log(p / q)).sum()

    @staticmethod
    def js_divergence(p, q, eps=1e-10):
        p = np.asarray(p) + eps
        q = np.asarray(q) + eps
        p = p / p.sum()
        q = q / q.sum()
        m = 0.5 * (p + q)
        return 0.5 * (p * np.log(p / m)).sum() + 0.5 * (q * np.log(q / m)).sum()

    def kl_per_dim(self, orig_emb, recon_emb, num_bins=50):
        d = orig_emb.shape[-1]
        kls = np.zeros(d)
        for i in range(d):
            p, edges = np.histogram(orig_emb[:, i].cpu().numpy(), bins=num_bins, density=True)
            q, _ = np.histogram(recon_emb[:, i].cpu().numpy(), bins=edges, density=True)
            p = p / (p.sum() + 1e-10)
            q = q / (q.sum() + 1e-10)
            mask = (p > 0) & (q > 0)
            kls[i] = (p[mask] * np.log(p[mask] / q[mask])).sum()
        return kls

    def information_loss(self, orig_emb, recon_emb):
        kls = self.kl_per_dim(orig_emb, recon_emb)
        mse = F.mse_loss(recon_emb, orig_emb).item()
        cosine = F.cosine_similarity(orig_emb, recon_emb, dim=-1).mean().item()
        return {
            'kl_mean': float(kls.mean()),
            'kl_max': float(kls.max()),
            'mse': mse,
            'cosine': cosine,
        }

    def evaluate(self, sids, orig_emb=None, recon_emb=None):
        from .usage_evaluator import SIDCodebookUsageEvaluator
        usage_eval = SIDCodebookUsageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = usage_eval.evaluate(sids)

        if orig_emb is not None and recon_emb is not None:
            result['information_loss'] = self.information_loss(orig_emb, recon_emb)

        return result
```

## A.7 下游任务模块

```python
"""下游任务模块"""
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Dict, Optional


class SIDDownstreamEvaluator:
    """SID 下游任务评估"""

    def __init__(self, num_items=10000):
        self.num_items = num_items

    def recall_at_k(self, predictions, targets, k=10):
        """
        predictions: [B, N] item 的预测分数
        targets: [B, K_target] 真实目标 item
        k: 检索 top-k
        """
        B = predictions.shape[0]
        topk = predictions.topk(k, dim=-1).indices  # [B, K]

        hits = 0
        total = 0
        for i in range(B):
            target_set = set(targets[i].cpu().tolist())
            topk_set = set(topk[i].cpu().tolist())
            hits += len(target_set & topk_set)
            total += len(target_set)
        return hits / total if total > 0 else 0

    def ndcg_at_k(self, predictions, targets, k=10):
        """NDCG@K"""
        B = predictions.shape[0]
        ndcgs = []
        for i in range(B):
            target_set = set(targets[i].cpu().tolist())
            topk = predictions[i].topk(k).indices.cpu().tolist()

            dcg = 0
            for j, item in enumerate(topk):
                if item in target_set:
                    dcg += 1 / np.log2(j + 2)

            idcg = sum(1 / np.log2(j + 2) for j in range(min(len(target_set), k)))
            ndcgs.append(dcg / idcg if idcg > 0 else 0)
        return float(np.mean(ndcgs))

    def auc(self, predictions, labels):
        """AUC: predictions vs labels"""
        from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score
        return roc_auc_score(labels.cpu().numpy(), predictions.cpu().numpy())

    def evaluate(self, predictions, targets, labels=None, k_list=(10, 50, 100)):
        result = {}
        for k in k_list:
            result[f'recall@{k}'] = self.recall_at_k(predictions, targets, k)
            result[f'ndcg@{k}'] = self.ndcg_at_k(predictions, targets, k)

        if labels is not None:
            result['auc'] = self.auc(predictions, labels)

        return result
```

## A.8 联合评估器

```python
"""联合评估器 (整合所有模块)"""
import numpy as np
import torch
from typing import Dict, Optional
import json
from datetime import datetime


class JointSIDEvaluator:
    """联合评估器"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers

        from .recon_evaluator import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        from .collision_coverage_evaluator import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        from .info_theory_evaluator import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator
        from .downstream_evaluator import SIDDownstreamEvaluator

        self.recon = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers)
        self.cc = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers)
        self.info = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(num_codes, num_layers)
        self.downstream = SIDDownstreamEvaluator()

    def evaluate(self, orig_emb, recon_emb, sids, popularity=None,
                 predictions=None, targets=None, labels=None):
        """主评估入口"""
        result = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'config': {
                'num_codes': self.num_codes,
                'num_layers': self.num_layers,
            },
            'reconstruction': self.recon.evaluate_recon(orig_emb, recon_emb, popularity),
            'collision_coverage': self.cc.evaluate(sids, popularity),
            'info_theory': self.info.evaluate(sids, orig_emb, recon_emb),
        }

        if predictions is not None and targets is not None:
            result['downstream'] = self.downstream.evaluate(predictions, targets, labels)

        result['overall_score'] = self._overall_score(result)
        result['verdict'] = self._verdict(result)

        return result

    def _overall_score(self, result):
        """综合评分"""
        score = 0
        if 'reconstruction' in result:
            score += 30 * result['reconstruction'].get('cosine_mean', 0)
        if 'collision_coverage' in result:
            cr = result['collision_coverage']['overall']['collision_rate']
            cov = result['collision_coverage']['overall']['coverage']
            score += 20 * max(0, 1 - cr * 5)
            score += 15 * cov
        if 'info_theory' in result:
            score += 20 * result['info_theory']['overall']['entropy_ratio']
        if 'downstream' in result:
            score += 15 * min(1, result['downstream'].get('recall@10', 0) * 5)
        return score

    def _verdict(self, result):
        s = result['overall_score']
        if s > 85: return 'EXCELLENT'
        if s > 70: return 'GOOD'
        if s > 50: return 'WARNING'
        return 'CRITICAL'
```

## A.9 监控与告警

```python
"""监控与告警"""
import numpy as np
import torch
from collections import deque
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional


class SIDMonitor:
    """SID 实时监控"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes=256, num_layers=4,
                 alert_thresholds=None, window_size=100):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.window_size = window_size

        self.thresholds = alert_thresholds or {
            'collision_rate': 0.10,
            'coverage': 0.70,
            'cosine_mean': 0.93,
            'mse': 0.06,
            'entropy_ratio': 0.70,
        }

        self.history = deque(maxlen=window_size)
        self.alerts = []

    def update(self, sids, orig_emb=None, recon_emb=None):
        """更新一次状态"""
        # 计算指标
        from .collision_coverage_evaluator import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        from .info_theory_evaluator import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator

        cc = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        info = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)

        cc_result = cc.evaluate(sids)
        info_result = info.evaluate(sids, orig_emb, recon_emb)

        state = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'collision_rate': cc_result['overall']['collision_rate'],
            'coverage': cc_result['overall']['coverage'],
            'entropy_ratio': info_result['overall']['entropy_ratio'],
        }

        if orig_emb is not None and recon_emb is not None:
            state['mse'] = float(((orig_emb - recon_emb)**2).mean())
            state['cosine'] = float(
                torch.nn.functional.cosine_similarity(orig_emb, recon_emb, dim=-1).mean()
            )

        self.history.append(state)
        self._check_alerts(state)
        return state

    def _check_alerts(self, state):
        for k, threshold in self.thresholds.items():
            if k not in state:
                continue
            value = state[k]

            if k in ['collision_rate', 'mse']:
                # 高优越大越差
                if value > threshold:
                    self.alerts.append({
                        'timestamp': state['timestamp'],
                        'metric': k,
                        'value': value,
                        'threshold': threshold,
                        'level': 'CRITICAL' if value > threshold * 1.5 else 'WARNING',
                    })
            else:
                # 低优越小越差
                if value < threshold:
                    self.alerts.append({
                        'timestamp': state['timestamp'],
                        'metric': k,
                        'value': value,
                        'threshold': threshold,
                        'level': 'CRITICAL' if value < threshold * 0.85 else 'WARNING',
                    })

    def get_recent_alerts(self, n=10):
        return self.alerts[-n:]

    def detect_drift(self):
        """检测漂移"""
        if len(self.history) < self.window_size // 2:
            return None

        recent = list(self.history)[-self.window_size // 2:]
        past = list(self.history)[:self.window_size // 2]

        for metric in ['collision_rate', 'coverage', 'cosine', 'mse', 'entropy_ratio']:
            recent_vals = [s[metric] for s in recent if metric in s]
            past_vals = [s[metric] for s in past if metric in s]
            if not recent_vals or not past_vals:
                continue

            recent_mean = np.mean(recent_vals)
            past_mean = np.mean(past_vals)
            drift_pct = abs(recent_mean - past_mean) / (past_mean + 1e-8) * 100

            if drift_pct > 15:
                return {
                    'metric': metric,
                    'past_mean': past_mean,
                    'recent_mean': recent_mean,
                    'drift_pct': drift_pct,
                }
        return None
```

## A.10 报告生成器

```python
"""报告生成器"""
import json
import numpy as np
import torch
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Optional
import os


class SIDReportGenerator:
    """SID 评估报告生成"""

    def __init__(self, output_dir='./reports'):
        self.output_dir = output_dir
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

    def generate(self, evaluation_result: Dict, name: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        """生成报告"""
        if name is None:
            name = f"sid_eval_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"

        # 转 numpy 为 list
        def convert(o):
            if isinstance(o, np.ndarray):
                return o.tolist()
            if isinstance(o, (np.integer, np.floating)):
                return float(o)
            if isinstance(o, torch.Tensor):
                return o.tolist()
            if isinstance(o, np.bool_):
                return bool(o)
            return str(o)

        path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, f"{name}.json")
        with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(evaluation_result, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=convert)

        return path

    def generate_markdown(self, evaluation_result: Dict, path: str):
        """生成 Markdown 报告"""
        lines = ['# SID 评估报告\n']
        lines.append(f"**时间**: {evaluation_result.get('timestamp', 'N/A')}\n")
        lines.append(f"**配置**: num_codes={evaluation_result.get('config', {}).get('num_codes', 'N/A')}, "
                     f"num_layers={evaluation_result.get('config', {}).get('num_layers', 'N/A')}\n")
        lines.append(f"**综合评分**: {evaluation_result.get('overall_score', 'N/A'):.2f}\n")
        lines.append(f"**评估结论**: {evaluation_result.get('verdict', 'N/A')}\n\n")

        # 重建指标
        if 'reconstruction' in evaluation_result:
            lines.append("## 重建指标\n\n")
            recon = evaluation_result['reconstruction']
            lines.append(f"- MSE: {recon.get('mse', 0):.4f}\n")
            lines.append(f"- Cosine Mean: {recon.get('cosine_mean', 0):.4f}\n")
            lines.append(f"- Cosine P10: {recon.get('cosine_p10', 0):.4f}\n")
            if 'np@10' in recon:
                lines.append(f"- NP@10: {recon.get('np@10', 0):.4f}\n")
            lines.append("\n")

        # 碰撞/覆盖率
        if 'collision_coverage' in evaluation_result:
            lines.append("## 碰撞/覆盖率\n\n")
            cc = evaluation_result['collision_coverage']['overall']
            lines.append(f"- 碰撞率: {cc.get('collision_rate', 0):.2%}\n")
            lines.append(f"- 覆盖率: {cc.get('coverage', 0):.2%}\n")
            lines.append(f"- Top-10 集中度: {cc.get('top10_concentration', 0):.4f}\n\n")

        # 信息论指标
        if 'info_theory' in evaluation_result:
            lines.append("## 信息论指标\n\n")
            info = evaluation_result['info_theory']['overall']
            lines.append(f"- 熵率: {info.get('entropy_ratio', 0):.4f}\n")
            lines.append(f"- Gini: {info.get('gini', 0):.4f}\n")
            lines.append(f"- 有效码本大小: {info.get('effective_codebook_size', 0):.1f}\n\n")

        # 下游指标
        if 'downstream' in evaluation_result:
            lines.append("## 下游指标\n\n")
            ds = evaluation_result['downstream']
            for k, v in ds.items():
                lines.append(f"- {k}: {v:.4f}\n")
            lines.append("\n")

        with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.writelines(lines)

        return path
```

## A.11 完整使用示例

```python
"""完整使用示例"""
import torch
import numpy as np
from sid_evaluator import JointSIDEvaluator, SIDMonitor, SIDReportGenerator

## 准备数据
N = 10000
d = 128
num_codes = 256
num_layers = 4

orig_emb = torch.randn(N, d)
recon_emb = orig_emb + torch.randn(N, d) * 0.1
sids = torch.randint(0, num_codes, (N, num_layers))
popularity = np.random.pareto(1.0, N)

## 1. 离线评估
evaluator = JointSIDEvaluator(num_codes=num_codes, num_layers=num_layers)
result = evaluator.evaluate(
    orig_emb=orig_emb,
    recon_emb=recon_emb,
    sids=sids,
    popularity=popularity,
)

print(f"综合评分: {result['overall_score']:.2f}")
print(f"评估结论: {result['verdict']}")

## 2. 监控
monitor = SIDMonitor(num_codes=num_codes, num_layers=num_layers)
for i in range(10):
    state = monitor.update(sids, orig_emb, recon_emb)
    alerts = monitor.get_recent_alerts()
    if alerts:
        print(f"[ALERT] {alerts[-1]}")

## 3. 报告生成
reporter = SIDReportGenerator('./reports')
json_path = reporter.generate(result, name='my_sid_eval')
md_path = reporter.generate_markdown(result, './reports/my_sid_eval.md')
print(f"报告: {json_path}, {md_path}")

## A.12 单元测试

```python
"""单元测试"""
import unittest
import torch
import numpy as np
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')


class TestSIDEvaluator(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.num_codes = 16
        self.num_layers = 2
        self.N = 100
        self.d = 32

        # 完美重建
        self.orig = torch.randn(self.N, self.d)
        self.recon = self.orig.clone()

        # 均匀 SID
        self.sids_uniform = torch.zeros(self.N, self.num_layers, dtype=torch.long)
        for i in range(self.N):
            self.sids_uniform[i] = torch.tensor([i // 8, i % 8])

        # 坍缩 SID
        self.sids_collapse = torch.zeros(self.N, self.num_layers, dtype=torch.long)

    def test_reconstruction_mse(self):
        from sid_reconstruction_metrics import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = evaluator.reconstruction_mse(self.orig, self.recon)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result['mse'], 0, places=6)

    def test_cosine_perfect(self):
        from sid_reconstruction_metrics import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = evaluator.cosine_metrics(self.orig, self.recon)
        self.assertGreater(result['cosine_mean'], 0.999)

    def test_collision_rate_collapse(self):
        from sid_collision_coverage_metrics import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        cr = evaluator.full_collision_rate(self.sids_collapse)
        self.assertGreater(cr, 0.9)

    def test_coverage_uniform(self):
        from sid_collision_coverage_metrics import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        cov = evaluator.coverage(self.sids_uniform)
        self.assertGreater(cov, 0.5)

    def test_entropy_uniform(self):
        from sid_info_theory_metrics import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = evaluator.evaluate(self.sids_uniform)
        self.assertGreater(result['overall']['entropy_ratio'], 0.7)

    def test_entropy_collapse(self):
        from sid_info_theory_metrics import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator
        evaluator = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = evaluator.evaluate(self.sids_collapse)
        self.assertLess(result['overall']['entropy_ratio'], 0.5)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
```

## A.13 性能优化

### A.13.1 大数据集评估

```python
class FastSIDEvaluator:
    """快速评估器 (大数据集优化)"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes, num_layers, batch_size=10000):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.batch_size = batch_size

    def fast_collision_rate(self, sids):
        """O(N) 时间"""
        # 使用 numpy 优化
        if isinstance(sids, torch.Tensor):
            sids = sids.numpy()

        # 转换为字节 (假设每层 < 256)
        sids_bytes = sids.astype(np.uint8)

        # 把每行转为一个 unique key
        keys = sids_bytes.view([('', sids_bytes.dtype)] * sids_bytes.shape[1]).flatten()

        unique, counts = np.unique(keys, return_counts=True)
        n_collisions = (counts[counts > 1] - 1).sum()
        return n_collisions / len(sids)

    def fast_coverage(self, sids):
        """O(N) 时间"""
        if isinstance(sids, torch.Tensor):
            sids = sids.numpy()
        used = np.unique(sids.flatten())
        return len(used) / (self.num_codes * self.num_layers)
```

### A.13.2 GPU 加速

```python
class GPUSIDEvaluator:
    """GPU 加速的评估"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes, num_layers, device='cuda'):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.device = device

    def gpu_collision_rate(self, sids):
        sids = sids.to(self.device)
        # 用 torch.unique_consecutive + return_counts
        # 注: unique 在 GPU 上可能慢, 视情况用 CPU
        unique, counts = torch.unique(sids, dim=0, return_counts=True)
        n_collisions = (counts[counts > 1] - 1).sum()
        return (n_collisions / len(sids)).item()
```

### A.13.3 缓存策略

```python
class CachedEvaluator:
    """缓存评估结果"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.cache = {}

    def get_or_compute(self, key, compute_fn):
        if key in self.cache:
            return self.cache[key]
        result = compute_fn()
        self.cache[key] = result
        return result

    def clear(self):
        self.cache.clear()
```

## A.14 工业部署建议

### A.14.1 离线评估服务

```python
"""离线评估服务"""
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
import torch

app = Flask(__name__)

## 初始化评估器 (启动时加载)
evaluator = JointSIDEvaluator(num_codes=256, num_layers=4)

@app.route('/evaluate', methods=['POST'])
def evaluate():
    data = request.json

    orig_emb = torch.tensor(data['orig_emb'])
    recon_emb = torch.tensor(data['recon_emb'])
    sids = torch.tensor(data['sids'])
    popularity = data.get('popularity')

    result = evaluator.evaluate(orig_emb, recon_emb, sids, popularity)
    return jsonify(result)


@app.route('/health', methods=['GET'])
def health():
    return jsonify({'status': 'ok'})


if __name__ == '__main__':
    app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=5000)
```

### A.14.2 在线监控服务

```python
"""在线监控服务"""
import time
from kafka import KafkaConsumer
from kafka import KafkaProducer

monitor = SIDMonitor(num_codes=256, num_layers=4)
producer = KafkaProducer(bootstrap_servers='localhost:9092')

consumer = KafkaConsumer('sid_events', bootstrap_servers='localhost:9092')

for message in consumer:
    event = json.loads(message.value)
    sids = torch.tensor(event['sids'])

    state = monitor.update(sids)
    alerts = monitor.get_recent_alerts()

    if alerts:
        for alert in alerts:
            producer.send('sid_alerts', json.dumps(alert).encode())
```

### A.14.3 与 MLflow 集成

```python
"""MLflow 集成"""
import mlflow


def log_to_mlflow(result):
    """记录到 MLflow"""
    with mlflow.start_run():
        # 配置
        mlflow.log_param('num_codes', result['config']['num_codes'])
        mlflow.log_param('num_layers', result['config']['num_layers'])

        # 重建指标
        for k, v in result['reconstruction'].items():
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                mlflow.log_metric(f'reconstruction_{k}', v)

        # 碰撞/覆盖
        for k, v in result['collision_coverage']['overall'].items():
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                mlflow.log_metric(f'cc_{k}', v)

        # 信息论
        for k, v in result['info_theory']['overall'].items():
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                mlflow.log_metric(f'info_{k}', v)

        # 综合
        mlflow.log_metric('overall_score', result['overall_score'])

## A.18 工具函数 (utils.py)

```python
"""工具函数"""
import torch
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple


def to_numpy(x):
    """Tensor / numpy 统一转 numpy"""
    if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor):
        return x.detach().cpu().numpy()
    return np.asarray(x)


def gini_coefficient(x):
    """Gini 系数"""
    x = np.sort(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))
    n = len(x)
    if x.sum() == 0:
        return 0
    cum = np.cumsum(x)
    return (n + 1 - 2 * np.sum(cum) / cum[-1]) / n


def percentile_metrics(values, percentiles=(10, 50, 90, 99)):
    """分位数指标"""
    return {f'p{p}': float(np.percentile(values, p)) for p in percentiles}


def safe_divide(a, b, eps=1e-8):
    """安全除法"""
    return a / (b + eps)


def convert_to_serializable(obj):
    """转 numpy/torch 为可序列化"""
    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):
        return obj.tolist()
    if isinstance(obj, torch.Tensor):
        return obj.detach().cpu().tolist()
    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer, np.floating)):
        return float(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, np.bool_):
        return bool(obj)
    return str(obj)


def check_sids_valid(sids, num_codes):
    """检查 SID 合法性"""
    if sids.min() < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"SID 含负值: {sids.min()}")
    if sids.max() >= num_codes:
        raise ValueError(f"SID 超出码本大小: max={sids.max()} >= {num_codes}")
    return True


def merge_dicts(*dicts):
    """合并字典"""
    result = {}
    for d in dicts:
        result.update(d)
    return result


def flatten_dict(d, parent_key='', sep='_'):
    """扁平化字典"""
    items = []
    for k, v in d.items():
        new_key = f"{parent_key}{sep}{k}" if parent_key else k
        if isinstance(v, dict):
            items.extend(flatten_dict(v, new_key, sep).items())
        else:
            items.append((new_key, v))
    return dict(items)
```

## A.19 高级特性: 并行评估

```python
"""并行评估"""
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor
import multiprocessing as mp


class ParallelSIDEvaluator:
    """并行评估器"""

    def __init__(self, num_codes, num_layers, n_workers=4):
        self.num_codes = num_codes
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.n_workers = n_workers

    def parallel_evaluate(self, sids_list, orig_emb_list, recon_emb_list):
        """多份数据并行评估"""
        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.n_workers) as executor:
            futures = [
                executor.submit(self._eval_one, sids, orig, recon)
                for sids, orig, recon in zip(sids_list, orig_emb_list, recon_emb_list)
            ]
            return [f.result() for f in futures]

    def _eval_one(self, sids, orig_emb, recon_emb):
        """单份评估"""
        from sid_evaluator import JointSIDEvaluator
        evaluator = JointSIDEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        return evaluator.evaluate(orig_emb, recon_emb, sids)
```

## A.20 高级特性: 自定义指标

```python
"""自定义指标"""
class CustomMetricRegistry:
    """自定义指标注册表"""

    _registry = {}

    @classmethod
    def register(cls, name):
        """装饰器: 注册自定义指标"""
        def decorator(func):
            cls._registry[name] = func
            return func
        return decorator

    @classmethod
    def compute(cls, name, *args, **kwargs):
        if name not in cls._registry:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown metric: {name}")
        return cls._registry[name](*args, **kwargs)


## 使用示例
@CustomMetricRegistry.register('domain_specific_cosine')
def domain_specific_cosine(orig_emb, recon_emb, weights=None):
    """加权的 cosine (领域特定维度)"""
    cos = torch.nn.functional.cosine_similarity(orig_emb, recon_emb, dim=-1)
    if weights is not None:
        cos = (cos * weights).sum() / weights.sum()
    return cos.item()


@CustomMetricRegistry.register('category_separation')
def category_separation(sids, categories, num_codes):
    """类别间 SID 分离度"""
    intra_dist = []
    inter_dist = []
    for cat in np.unique(categories):
        mask = categories == cat
        sids_cat = sids[mask]
        if len(sids_cat) < 2:
            continue
        # 同类 SID 距离
        diffs = (sids_cat.unsqueeze(0) - sids_cat.unsqueeze(1)).abs().sum(-1)
        triu_idx = torch.triu_indices(diffs.shape[0], diffs.shape[1], offset=1)
        intra_dist.extend(diffs[triu_idx[0], triu_idx[1]].tolist())

    return float(np.mean(intra_dist)) if intra_dist else 0
```

## A.22 错误处理与日志

```python
"""错误处理与日志"""
import logging
from typing import Optional

## 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
)
logger = logging.getLogger('sid_evaluator')


class SafeEvaluator:
    """带错误处理的评估器"""

    def __init__(self, evaluator):
        self.evaluator = evaluator

    def safe_evaluate(self, *args, **kwargs) -> Optional[dict]:
        """带错误处理的评估"""
        try:
            return self.evaluator.evaluate(*args, **kwargs)
        except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
            logger.error("GPU OOM, 尝试 CPU fallback")
            # 切换到 CPU
            kwargs['device'] = 'cpu'
            return self.evaluator.evaluate(*args, **kwargs)
        except ValueError as e:
            logger.error(f"值错误: {e}")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception(f"未知错误: {e}")
            return None


## 使用
safe = SafeEvaluator(evaluator)
result = safe.safe_evaluate(orig_emb, recon_emb, sids)
if result is None:
    logger.error("评估失败, 跳过")
```

"""
Case: 一个新 SID 的完整评估流程

## 1. 准备数据
from sid_implementation.data import load_item_embeddings
embeds = load_item_embeddings('amazon_beauty')  # [12000, 128]
popularity = load_popularity('amazon_beauty')

## 2. 加载模型
from sid_implementation.models import RQVAE
model = RQVAE.load_from_checkpoint('checkpoints/rqvae_v1.ckpt')

## 3. 评估
from sid_evaluation import JointSIDEvaluator, SIDMonitor, SIDReportGenerator

evaluator = JointSIDEvaluator(num_codes=256, num_layers=4)

## 4. 离线评估
result = evaluator.evaluate(
    orig_emb=embeds['test']['embedding'],
    recon_emb=None,  # 由 evaluator 计算
    sids=None,
    popularity=popularity['test'],
    model=model,
)

## 5. 在线监控 (实时数据)
monitor = SIDMonitor(num_codes=256, num_layers=4)
## ... 持续更新 ...

## 6. 生成报告
reporter = SIDReportGenerator('./reports')
json_path = reporter.generate(result, name='rqvae_v1')
md_path = reporter.generate_markdown(result, './reports/rqvae_v1.md')

## 7. 决策

def verify_codebook_compatibility(model, expected_codebook_path):
    """验证码本版本"""
    expected = torch.load(expected_codebook_path)
    actual = model.codebook.state_dict()

    for k in expected:
        if k not in actual:
            raise ValueError(f"码本 key 缺失: {k}")
        if not torch.allclose(expected[k], actual[k], atol=1e-3):
            raise ValueError(f"码本值不匹配: {k}")
```

def handle_nan_metrics(metrics):
    """处理 NaN 指标"""
    for k in list(metrics.keys()):
        v = metrics[k]
        if isinstance(v, float) and np.isnan(v):
            metrics[k] = None
            print(f"[WARN] {k} 是 NaN")
    return metrics

## A.30 单元测试覆盖

### A.30.1 完整测试套件

```python
"""test_sid_evaluation.py"""
import unittest
import torch
import numpy as np
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, '.')

class TestAll(unittest.TestCase):

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.num_codes = 32
        cls.num_layers = 3
        cls.N = 1000
        cls.d = 64

        np.random.seed(42)
        torch.manual_seed(42)

        cls.orig = torch.randn(cls.N, cls.d)
        cls.recon = cls.orig + torch.randn(cls.N, cls.d) * 0.05
        cls.sids = torch.randint(0, cls.num_codes, (cls.N, cls.num_layers))
        cls.popularity = np.random.pareto(1.0, cls.N) + 0.1

    def test_01_mse(self):
        from sid_recon_evaluator import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        ev = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = ev.reconstruction_mse(self.orig, self.recon)
        self.assertLess(result['mse'], 0.01)

    def test_02_cosine(self):
        from sid_recon_evaluator import SIDReconstructionEvaluator
        ev = SIDReconstructionEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = ev.cosine_metrics(self.orig, self.recon)
        self.assertGreater(result['cosine_mean'], 0.9)

    def test_03_collision(self):
        from sid_collision_coverage_evaluator import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        ev = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        cr = ev.full_collision_rate(self.sids)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(cr, 0)
        self.assertLessEqual(cr, 1)

    def test_04_coverage(self):
        from sid_collision_coverage_evaluator import SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator
        ev = SIDCollisionCoverageEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        cov = ev.coverage(self.sids)
        self.assertGreater(cov, 0.3)

    def test_05_entropy(self):
        from sid_info_theory_evaluator import SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator
        ev = SIDInfoTheoryEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = ev.evaluate(self.sids)
        self.assertGreater(result['overall']['entropy_ratio'], 0.5)

    def test_06_joint(self):
        from sid_evaluator import JointSIDEvaluator
        ev = JointSIDEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = ev.evaluate(self.orig, self.recon, self.sids, self.popularity)
        self.assertIn('overall_score', result)
        self.assertIn('verdict', result)

    def test_07_monitor(self):
        from sid_monitor import SIDMonitor
        monitor = SIDMonitor(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        for _ in range(5):
            monitor.update(self.sids, self.orig, self.recon)
        alerts = monitor.get_recent_alerts()
        self.assertIsInstance(alerts, list)

    def test_08_reporter(self):
        from sid_evaluator import JointSIDEvaluator
        from sid_reporter import SIDReportGenerator
        ev = JointSIDEvaluator(self.num_codes, self.num_layers)
        result = ev.evaluate(self.orig, self.recon, self.sids, self.popularity)

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
            reporter = SIDReportGenerator(tmpdir)
            path = reporter.generate(result, name='test')
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(path))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
```
